=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
You are given positive integers N, K, and an integer sequence of length N: A = (A_1, A_2, \dots, A_N).
Find \displaystyle \sum_{1\leq l\leq r\leq N} \Bigg(\sum_{l\leq i\leq r} A_i\Bigg)^K, modulo 998244353.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N K
A_1 A_2 \dots A_N

Output

Print the answer.

Constraints


- 1\leq N \leq 2\times 10^5
- 1\leq K \leq 10
- 0 \leq A_i < 998244353
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

3 2
3 1 2

Sample Output 1

75

The value is A_1^2+A_2^2+A_3^2+(A_1+A_2)^2+(A_2+A_3)^2+(A_1+A_2+A_3)^2=3^2+1^2+2^2+4^2+3^2+6^2=75.

Sample Input 2

1 10
0

Sample Output 2

0

Sample Input 3

10 5
91 59 85 60 57 72 12 3 27 16

Sample Output 3

428633385

Be sure to find the sum modulo 998244353.

```

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- spending too long comparing nearby plans before committing to one workable approach;
- returning from code to broad algorithm discussion without a concrete contradiction;
- turning one local doubt into a full restart when the current draft only needs a small patch.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Pseudo:

import sys
MOD=998244353
def main():
    input=sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N,K=map(int,input().split())
    A=list(map(int,input().split()))
    # prefix
    cnt=[0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0]=1  # prefix 0
    ans=0
    pref=0
    # precompute comb
    comb=[[0]*(K+1) for _ in range(K+1)]
    for n in range(K+1):
        comb[n][0]=comb[n][n]=1
        for r in range(1,n):
            comb[n][r]=(comb[n-1][r-1]+comb[n-1][r])%MOD
    for a in A:
        pref=(pref+a)%MOD
        x=pref
        # pow_x
        px=[1]*(K+1)
        for e in range(1,K+1):
            px[e]=px[e-1]*x%MOD
        # contribution
        # Need for m 0..K: comb[K][m]*px[K-m]*cnt[m]
        # Could loop m
        for m in range(K+1):
            ans = (ans + comb[K][m] * px[K-m] % MOD * cnt[m]) % MOD
        # update cnt with -x
        neg = (-x) % MOD
        pn=[1]*(K+1)
        for e in range(1,K+1):
            pn[e]=pn[e-1]*neg%MOD
        for m in range(K+1):
            cnt[m] = (cnt[m] + pn[m]) % MOD
    print(ans%MOD)

Let's produce final code:

import sys
MOD = 998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data: return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    # binomial coefficients
    C = [[0]*(K+1) for _ in range(K+1)]
    for n in range(K+1):
        C[n][0] = C[n][n] = 1
        for r in range(1, n):
            C[n][r] = (C[n-1][r-1] + C[n-1][r]) % MOD

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1  # prefix 0
    ans = 0
    pref = 0

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px = [1]*(K+1)
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        row = C[K]
        # add contributions for subarrays ending here
        for m in range(K+1):
            ans = (ans + row[m] * px[K-m] % MOD * cnt[m]) % MOD

        neg = (-x) % MOD
        pn = [1]*(K+1)
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            pn[e] = pn[e-1] * neg % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            cnt[m] = (cnt[m] + pn[m]) % MOD

    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Then code:
MOD = 998244353

def main():
    import sys
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K - m + 1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px = [1]*(K+1)
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        # contribution
        # ans += Σ comb[m] * x^(K-m) * cnt[m]
        # local variables for speed?
        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        neg = (-x) % MOD
        pn = [1]*(K+1)
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            pn[e] = pn[e-1] * neg % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            v = cnt[m] + pn[m]
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
import sys

MOD = 998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2 + N]

    comb = [1] * (K + 1)
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        comb[m] = comb[m - 1] * (K - m + 1) // m

    cnt = [0] * (K + 1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px = [1] * (K + 1)
        for e in range(1, K + 1):
            px[e] = px[e - 1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K + 1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K - m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        neg = (-x) % MOD
        pn = [1] * (K + 1)
        for e in range(1, K + 1):
            pn[e] = pn[e - 1] * neg % MOD

        for m in range(K + 1):
            v = cnt[m] + pn[m]
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

...

    x = pref

    px[0] = 1
    pn[0] = 1

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        ans %= MOD

            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:

            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)

    if m & 1:
        val = MOD - val if val else 0
    v = cnt[m] + val
    if v >= MOD: v -= MOD
    cnt[m] = v
```
This avoids pn list and neg power loop. But note `px[m]` is x^m mod. For m odd, (-x)^m = -x^m mod. If val=0, MOD - 0 = MOD, need 0. So:
```
val = px[m]
if m & 1:
    val = (-val) % MOD
```
Using `%` for odd m only, 5 times per iteration ->1M mods, okay. Or branch:
```
if m & 1:
    val = 0 if val == 0 else MOD - val
```
This is fast. Then we don't need `neg` or `pn` at all. But is `(-x)^m` equal to `(-1)^m x^m` in modulo ring? Yes. Since x is residue of prefix mod M. So update cnt with val. This reduces power loop from 10 to 5? Actually px loop still 10. pn loop eliminated. Contribution loop 11, update loop 11. Total operations per r: px 10 + contrib 11 + update 11 =32, same as combined px/pn loop? Combined px/pn loop was 10 iterations with two multiplications =20 multiplications, plus contrib 11 + update 11 =42 loop iterations? Wait loop iterations count: combined power loop 10, contribution 11, update 11 =32 iterations, but power loop has 2 multiplications. Let's verify correctness carefully.

For each previous prefix y, we need add (-y)^m to cnt[m]. We store y mod M = x? Actually for current prefix y = pref, we have px[m] = y^m mod M. Then (-y)^m mod M = (-1)^m * y^m mod M. If m even, same; if m odd, -y^m mod M = (MOD - y^m) mod M. Since px[m] in [0,MOD-1], if px[m]=0, MOD - 0 = MOD, need 0. Use `if m & 1: val = 0 if val == 0 else MOD - val`. This is correct. Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

px = [0]*(K+1)
pn = [0]*(K+1)
...

    pref = (pref + a) % MOD
    x = pref

    px[0] = 1
    pn[0] = 1

```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r2 x=4 px [1,4,16]; contribution 17; update cnt1: val=MOD-4, cnt1=2MOD-7 -> MOD-7. Good.
r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

px = [0]*(K+1)
pn = [0]*(K+1)
...

    x = pref
    neg = (-x) % MOD
    px[0] = 1
    pn[0] = 1

```

```

```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```
Let's test sample 1 with this code:
r1 x=3 px [1,3,9]; contribution 9; update cnt0=2, cnt1=MOD-3, cnt2=9.
r2 x=4 px [1,4,16]; contribution 17; update cnt1: val=MOD-4, cnt1=2MOD-7 -> MOD-7. Good.

px = [0]*(K+1)
pn = [0]*(K+1)
...

    pref = (pref + a) % MOD
    x = pref
    neg = (-x) % MOD
    px[0] = 1
    pn[0] = 1
    for e in range(1, K+1):

    cnt[m] = v

```

```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

    if v >= MOD:
        v -= MOD
    cnt[m] = v
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

r3 x=6 px [1,6,36]; contribution 49. Good.

MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:

Now, code:
```
import sys
MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

        px[0] = 1
        for e in range(1, K+1):
            px[e] = px[e-1] * x % MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            ans += comb[m] * px[K-m] * cnt[m]
        ans %= MOD

        for m in range(K+1):
            val = px[m]
            if m & 1 and val:
                val = MOD - val
            v = cnt[m] + val
            if v >= MOD:
                v -= MOD
            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
```

MOD=998244353

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N, K = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]

    comb = [1]*(K+1)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        comb[m] = comb[m-1] * (K-m+1) // m

    cnt = [0]*(K+1)
    cnt[0] = 1
    ans = 0
    pref = 0
    px = [0]*(K+1)

    for a in A:
        pref = (pref + a) % MOD
        x = pref

            cnt[m] = v

    print(ans)
